Unveiling the mechanisms behind this emergence is critical, but the disconnection between simple rewards and collective behaviors exacerbates interpretability challenges. This paper aims to reveal the hidden mechanisms in this process. We propose a two-stage EEC (\LinkIII) explanatory framework. This includes a novel analytical tool called the Agent Response Map (ARM), which reveals agents' decision-making patterns across space and identifies regions of aggregation and avoidance. ARM reveals that the robots implicitly learn the geometric fields of the environment and utilize these structures as desired targets for coordinated movement. We validate this finding across two distinct tasks: a cooperative multi-robot shape assembly and a competitive predator-prey pursuit-evasion. 1) In the cooperative task, ARM identifies the unoccupied target interior as the desired destination for robot navigation. As the center becomes occupied, this target region automatically shifts toward the boundary, demonstrating the robots' capacity to autonomously explore unoccupied areas. 2) In the competitive task, ARM surprisingly identifies the boundary of the predators' Voronoi diagram as the convergence destination for prey agents. Together, these two tasks demonstrate the capability of ARM to discover the hidden geometric structures underlying MARL policies in robot swarms.

</details>


### [125] [UR-VC: Unsupervised Robotic Value Correction for Time-Derived Progress Proxies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12892)
*Lirui Zhao,Modi Shi,Li Chen,Qi Liu,Ping Luo,Hongyang Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出无监督机器人价值校正（UR-VC），一种无需训练、离线的时序标签校正方法。通过跨轨迹检索相似状态并聚合其时间标签，修正接触密集型操作中时间标签无法反映实际进展的问题。在真实双臂布匹折叠数据上验证，校正后的标签能捕捉局部进展倒退和非均匀进展，并提升VLA策略训练的成功率趋势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代机器人学习系统依赖密集的进度或价值信号来评估中间状态、指导策略学习和检测任务完成。由于此类密集标签难以大规模获取，归一化时间常被用作替代指标。然而，在接触密集型操作中，机器人可能会经历进展后退（如滑动、抓取失败），而时间标签仍单调递增，导致信号噪声过大。

Method: UR-VC是一种离线、无需训练的方法。其核心洞察是：相似状态常在不同演示片段中重复出现，但处于不同时间戳。UR-VC不信任单一轨迹的时间戳，而是跨片段检索相似状态，聚合它们的时间标签以获得校正后的进度估计。无需手动进度标签、奖励标注或额外的价值模型。

Result: 在真实双臂布匹折叠与展开数据上验证，校正标签能够捕捉归一化时间无法表示的局部回归和非均匀进展，同时保留整体任务趋势。基于校正信号构建优势标签用于VLA训练，在匹配数据、模型和训练设置的条件下，真实机器人任务成功率呈现正向趋势。

Conclusion: UR-VC通过跨轨迹状态检索与标签聚合，有效修正了接触密集型操作中时间标签的噪声问题，为密集进度信号的获取提供了一种低成本、无需训练的解决方案，并初步验证了其在策略学习中的正向作用。

Abstract: Modern robot learning systems increasingly rely on dense progress or value signals to evaluate intermediate states, guide policy learning, and detect task completion, making the quality of these signals critical. Since such dense labels are rarely available at scale, normalized time within a demonstration is often used as a scalable substitute: later frames are treated as higher progress. However, this time-derived label is only a noisy proxy for physical task progress. In contact-rich manipulation, a robot may make progress and then lose it through slips, failed grasps, or partial undoing, while the time-derived label continues to increase monotonically. We introduce Unsupervised Robotic Value Correction (UR-VC), an offline, training-free method for correcting time-derived progress labels. UR-VC exploits a simple regularity in demonstration data: similar states often recur across different episodes, but at different timestamps. Instead of trusting the timestamp from a single trajectory, UR-VC retrieves similar states from other episodes and aggregates their time-derived labels to obtain a corrected progress estimate. UR-VC requires no manual progress labels, reward annotations, or additional value model. We evaluate UR-VC on real bimanual cloth flatten-and-fold data, a long-horizon deformable-object manipulation task with visible intermediate progress. The corrected labels capture local regressions and non-uniform progress that normalized time cannot represent, while preserving the overall task trend. We further use the corrected signal to construct advantage labels for VLA training, following recent advantage-conditioned policy learning. UR-VC shows a positive trend in real-robot task success under matched data, model, and training settings.

</details>


### [126] [ExToken: Structured Exploration for Efficient Vision-Language-Action Reinforcement Fine-tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12931)
*Yilun Kong,Yunpeng Qing,Guozheng Ma,Haoyu Wang,Li Shen,Zhi Hou,Dacheng Tao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出RL探索令牌(ExToken)，通过将VLA策略条件化于离线演示中的离散行为先验，实现结构化探索，显著提升样本效率和任务性能，尤其在交互预算受限场景下表现出强鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前VLA-RL框架中探索停滞瓶颈严重，环境交互成本高昂限制了RL在VLA模型上的实际可扩展性。作者发现轨迹多样性比轨迹数量对样本效率更为关键。

Method: 提出ExToken框架：1) 从离线演示中提取离散行为先验作为探索令牌；2) 在 rollout 收集时条件化VLA策略于不同令牌，鼓励探索多样行为模式；3) 引入状态条件化令牌选择器，在部署时自适应预测有效行为模式。

Result: 在模拟和真实机器人操作任务上，ExToken持续加速收敛、提升任务性能，且在高度受限的交互预算下表现出强鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 轨迹多样性比轨迹数量对样本效率更关键，ExToken通过结构化探索有效缓解了VLA-RL中的探索停滞问题，是一种简单通用的框架。

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning (RL) has demonstrated significant potential for improving Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models on complex manipulation tasks. However, its practical scalability remains severely limited by the substantial cost of environmental interactions. In this work, we first investigate the exploration stagnation bottleneck in current VLA-RL frameworks and reveal that trajectory diversity is fundamentally more important to sample efficiency than the sheer quantity of collected rollouts. Motivated by these insights, we introduce RL Exploration Token (ExToken), a simple yet general framework that condition VLA policies on discrete behavioral priors derived from offline demonstrations for structured exploration. By conditioning the policy on different tokens during rollout collection, ExToken encourages the agent to explore diverse behavioral modes, substantially improving state-action coverage and exploration efficiency. To bridge exploration during training with deterministic inference at deployment, ExToken further incorporates a state-conditioned token selector that adaptively predicts effective behavioral modes for unseen scenarios. Extensive experiments across simulated and real-world robotic manipulation tasks demonstrate that ExToken consistently accelerates convergence, improves task performance, and exhibits strong robustness under highly constrained interaction budgets.

</details>


### [127] [MAMMOTH: A Multi-Modal End-to-End Policy for Off-Road Mobility Robust to Missing Modality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12965)
*Ahaan Kotian,Shivani Subramanyan,Suresh Sundaram*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: MAMMOTH是一种统一的端到端导航策略，通过多模态融合（RGB、热成像、3D点云、自车速度）和模态丢弃训练，结合扩散策略学习物理轨迹与内在可通行性启发式，实现了在非结构化越野环境（包括夜间）中鲁棒的视觉导航，显著提升了避障、地形感知规划和缺失模态泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有越野导航方法高度依赖RGB模态，在光照变化（眩光、阴影、低光）下表现不佳；多模态方法则刚性依赖近乎完美的传感器输入，无法鲁棒处理传感器退化或单模态失效问题。

Method: 提出MAMMOTH框架：1) 高效融合多模态观测（RGB、热成像、3D点云、自车速度）；2) 采用模态丢弃训练策略，使模型在推理时能泛化到缺失模态；3) 使用扩散策略学习物理可行的轨迹与内在可通行性启发式的联合条件概率分布，倾向于选择更安全、更平滑的轨迹。

Result: 在真实越野环境（包括夜间）的机器人实验中，MAMMOTH在碰撞避免、地形感知规划和缺失模态泛化方面显著优于现有方法。

Conclusion: MAMMOTH通过多模态融合与模态丢弃训练，有效解决了非结构化环境下光照变化和传感器退化带来的导航鲁棒性问题，为可靠越野自主导航提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: Reliable autonomous navigation in unstructured off-road environments remains a critical unsolved challenge due to extreme terrain diversity, drastic illumination variations and acute sensor degradation. Recent developments have approached the problem as a traversability costmap estimation or visual navigation task. However, many exhibit heavy reliance on RGB modality, leading to poor performance in varied illumination such as glares, shadows or low ambient light. Achieving robust generalization in such conditions requires integrating modalities that provide supplementary scene information. Such multi-modal methods suffer from a rigid dependency on the presence of near-perfect sensor inputs, leaving them unable to robustly handle sensor degradation or individual modality failure. To address these limitations, we introduce MAMMOTH (MAsking Multi-Modal inputs for Off-road Traversability Heuristic-informed navigation), a unified end-to-end navigation policy for robust off-road visual-goal-conditioned navigation and undirected exploration. Specifically, MAMMOTH efficiently fuses multi-modal observations (RGB, Thermal, 3D Pointcloud and Ego Velocity) and is trained with a modality dropout scheme, enabling it to generalize to missing modalities at inference time. Furthermore, we employ a diffusion policy to learn the joint conditional probability distribution of physically-grounded trajectories and a intrinsic traversability heuristic. MAMMOTH utilizes this heuristic to prefer safer, smoother trajectories. We validate MAMMOTH through extensive real-world robot experiments in distinct off-road environments, including night-time operation. Our results demonstrate superior performance, with significant improvements in collision avoidance, terrain-aware planning and generalization to missing modalities. The code and dataset used for this work will be made publicly available.

</details>


### [128] [ChunkFlow: Towards Continuity-Consistent Chunked Policy Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12992)
*Zhao Yang,Yinan Shi,Mingyuan Yao,Wenyao Xue,Yawei Jueluo,Longjun Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ChunkFlow提出了一种面向VLA模型分块动作头的接缝感知训练-执行框架，通过将分块划分为冻结、可编辑和未来区域，结合确定性重叠混合与连续性损失训练，解决分块边界抖动问题，在CALVIN、LIBERO和真实机器人上提升了成功率和稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型采用分块动作头满足实时性要求，但相邻分块重叠区域预测不一致产生边界抖动，降低时间连贯性和任务成功率；现有推理时混合方法仅重新加权错误预测而未纠正底层错误，在偏置或有噪声的历史下导致残差累积。

Method: 1) 将每个分块划分为冻结区、可编辑区和未来区；2) 执行时采用确定性重叠混合；3) 训练时引入接缝损失及一阶、二阶连续性损失；4) 历史损坏和计划采样增强对执行历史误差的鲁棒性；5) AWAC微调阶段在不移除结构正则化器的情况下适配策略。

Result: 在CALVIN、LIBERO和真实机器人实验上，ChunkFlow在低延迟推理下实现了更优的成功率-稳定性权衡；且在光滑性假设下，预混合接缝差异随重叠增加而衰减。

Conclusion: ChunkFlow通过结构化的分块对齐与接缝感知训练，有效缓解了分块动作头的边界抖动问题，提升了VLA模型的时间一致性和任务成功率。

Abstract: Vision-language action (VLA) models increasingly adopt chunked action heads to satisfy real-time constraints; however, this introduces boundary jitter: overlapping regions between consecutive chunks often yield inconsistent predictions, degrading temporal coherence and the task success rate. Existing methods, such as inference-time blending, merely reweight mismatched proposals without correcting underlying errors, leading to residual accumulation under biased or noisy histories. We propose ChunkFlow, a seam-aware training-and-execution framework for chunked policies that aligns chunk structure with boundary execution. It partitions each chunk into frozen, editable, and future zones, applies deterministic overlap blending at execution, and trains raw predictions with seam and first- and second-order continuity losses. History corruption and scheduled sampling improve robustness to executed-history errors, while an AWAC fine-tuning stage adapts the policy without removing these structural regularizers. Under mild smoothness assumptions, pre-blending seam discrepancies provably decay with increasing overlap. Experiments on CALVIN, LIBERO, and real robots show an improved success-stability trade-off with low-latency inference. Project page: https://cytoderm-ai.github.io/chunkflow.

</details>


### [129] [FlowWAM: Optical Flow as a Unified Action Representation for World Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13017)
*Yixiang Chen,Peiyan Li,Yuan Xu,Qisen Ma,Jiabing Yang,Kai Wang,Jianhua Yang,Dong An,He Guan,Gaoteng Liu,Jianlou Si,Jun Huang,Jing Liu,Nianfeng Liu,Yan Huang,Liang Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: FlowWAM提出了一种基于光流的统一视频原生动作表示方法，通过双流扩散框架同时实现世界建模和动作预测，在操控任务上达到92.94%的成功率，在世界建模上实现63.71的EWMScore。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数值动作表示难以与预训练视频生成器对齐，而视觉动作表示忽略了帧间的时序运动结构，因此需要一种既能对齐视频生成器又能携带充足运动线索的统一动作表示。

Method: 采用光流作为统一的视频原生动作表示，构建双流扩散框架FlowWAM，在共享的预训练视频生成器中联合建模RGB视频和光流视频，支持策略模式（生成光流进行动作预测）和世界模型模式（利用目标光流序列引导未来视频生成），并可从无动作标注的原始视频中提取光流进行预训练。

Result: 在RoboTwin操控任务上，Clean设置下成功率达92.94%，Random设置下达92.14%，优于VLA和WAM基线；在WorldArena世界建模上，最佳EWMScore为63.71，轨迹精度相对提升18.4%。

Conclusion: 基于光流的动作表示能够有效对齐预训练视频生成器并携带丰富的运动信息，在动作预测和世界建模两个模式下均取得显著性能提升，且支持无标注视频数据的预训练。

Abstract: World Action Models (WAMs) are able to leverage pretrained video generators for both world modeling and action prediction. However, directly leveraging such video generators for control raises a new challenge: how to represent actions in a suitable form that aligns with pretrained video generators while carrying enough motion cues for accurate control. Existing numerical actions fail to satisfy the former, and prior visual action representations overlook the temporal motion structure across frames. We address this issue with FlowWAM, a dual-stream diffusion framework that adopts optical flow as a unified, video-native action representation. Flow videos share the same format as RGB videos and encode rich per-pixel displacement. By jointly modeling them within a shared pretrained video generator, FlowWAM can naturally implement two modes of WAMs. In policy mode, FlowWAM generates flow for action prediction, while in world-model mode, it uses target flow sequences to guide future video generation. Moreover, since flow can be easily extracted from raw videos without action labels, FlowWAM can leverage large-scale action-unlabeled video datasets for pretraining. We empirically find that our flow-based action representation delivers gains across both modes. On RoboTwin manipulation, FlowWAM raises the success rate to 92.94% on the Clean setting and 92.14% on Random, outperforming both VLA and WAM baselines. On WorldArena world modeling, it achieves the best overall EWMScore (63.71) with an 18.4% relative improvement in trajectory accuracy. More results can be found on our project website: https://flow-wam.github.io .

</details>


### [130] [DenseReward: Dense Reward Learning via Failure Synthesis for Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13033)
*Yu Fang,Wanxi Dong,Jiaqi Liu,Yue Yang,Mingxiao Huo,Yao Mu,Huaxiu Yao,Li Erran Li,Daniel Szafir,Mingyu Ding*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出DenseReward，一种密集机器人奖励模型。通过自动化的仿真故障数据生成流水线合成多样化的物理故障轨迹（如碰撞、抓取失败、物体掉落等），无需人工标注。该模型能从视觉观察和语言指令预测密集的帧级奖励分数，实现对任务进度的细粒度估计。实验表明其在密集奖励预测上优于通用VLM和现有机器人奖励模型，并能有效为下游模型预测控制和强化学习提供奖励指导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习在实际机器人应用中受限于缺乏可靠且能提供密集反馈的视觉语言奖励模型。现有方法面临两大瓶颈：一是获取多样化故障数据需要大量人工劳动，二是现有奖励模型仅提供稀疏的轨迹级奖励，对策略优化指导有限。

Method: 1) 开发自动化故障数据生成流水线，在仿真环境中无需人工标注即可合成物理真实的故障轨迹，涵盖碰撞、抓取失败、物体掉落和恢复行为等多种故障模式。2) 提出DenseReward模型，从视觉观察和语言指令输入预测密集的帧级奖励分数，实现整个回合中任务进度的细粒度估计。

Result: DenseReward在密集奖励预测任务上优于通用VLMs和现有机器人奖励模型，在仿真和真实机器人操作场景中均表现优越。同时，该模型能为下游模型预测控制和强化学习提供有效的奖励指导。

Conclusion: 自动化故障数据生成结合密集帧级奖励建模是提升机器人强化学习奖励信号质量的有效路径。本文发布数据集、训练好的奖励模型和评估套件，以支持机器人学习中故障感知密集奖励建模的发展。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning holds great promise for improving robot policies beyond the limits of imitation learning. However, its practical adoption remains bottlenecked by the lack of reliable vision-language reward models that provide dense and informative feedback. Two key challenges remain: acquiring diverse failure data at scale and obtaining fine-grained reward signals beyond sparse trajectory-level success labels. Collecting failure trajectories typically requires laborious human effort, while pseudo-failures constructed by relabeling successful demonstrations fail to capture the diverse physical failure modes that arise during robot execution. Meanwhile, existing reward models often predict sparse binary or trajectory-level rewards, which provide limited guidance for efficient policy optimization. We introduce DenseReward, a dense robotic reward model that addresses both challenges. To train DenseReward, we develop an automated failure data generation pipeline that synthesizes physically realistic failure trajectories in simulation without human labeling, covering diverse failure modes such as collisions, missed grasps, object drops, and recovery behaviors. DenseReward predicts dense frame-level reward scores from visual observations and language instructions, enabling fine-grained estimation of task progress throughout an episode. Experiments show that DenseReward outperforms general-purpose VLMs and existing robotic reward models in dense reward prediction across both simulated and real-world manipulation. We further demonstrate that DenseReward provides effective reward guidance for downstream model predictive control and reinforcement learning. We release the dataset, trained reward models, and evaluation suite to support the development of failure-aware dense reward modeling for robot learning.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [131] [Optimal Adaptive Market Making: A Theoretical Framework for High-Yield Liquidity Provision in Perpetual Futures Markets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11888)
*Minmin Zeng,Yi Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文为永续合约做市商在零手续费环境下的最优策略建立了严格的理论框架，推导了PnL分解定理、HJB方程、主APY公式等核心结果，并通过数值分析揭示了盈利与亏损区域间的相变。该框架统一并扩展了Avellaneda-Stoikov、Gueant-Lehalle-Fernandez-Tapia和Glosten-Milgrom等经典范式到现代去中心化交易所微观结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 填补去中心化永续合约市场中零手续费做市商最优策略的理论空白，将现代Decentralized交易所特有的零手续费、资金费率、跨交易所对冲等特征纳入严格的最优控制框架，并统一传统做市理论以适应新市场微观结构。

Method: 将做市商问题建模为滤波概率空间上的随机最优控制问题，控制变量为适应性买卖价差和跨交易所库存对冲决策。采用CARA效用函数推导HJB方程并给出验证定理；通过五个无量纲参数刻画高APY区域；运用Bayesian自适应估计、Kelly准则和组合优化等方法处理库存分布、杠杆和组合分配问题。

Result: 提出了包含十项贡献的系统性理论结果：PnL分解定理、HJB方程、主APY公式、零手续费经济学分析、最优跨交易所对冲策略（三分法）、稳健性边际、指数衰减回撤概率界与普适APV-VaR恒等式、遍历库存分布、Kelly最优杠杆与破产边界、多资产组合分配与分散化饱和结果。23张数值分析图表揭示了盈利与非盈利区域间的相变。

Conclusion: 本文为去中心化永续合约市场的做市商提供了完整的最优策略理论，统一了经典做市模型在现代Decentralized微观结构下的扩展，对实践中的做市策略设计具有指导意义。

Abstract: We develop a rigorous theoretical framework for optimal market making in perpetual futures markets with zero maker fees. We model the market maker's problem as a stochastic optimal control problem on a filtered probability space, where the controls are adaptive bid-ask spreads and inventory hedging decisions across two exchanges. Our contributions include: (i) a PnL decomposition theorem separating revenue into spread income, adverse selection loss, inventory carrying cost, hedging friction, and funding rate exposure; (ii) the Hamilton-Jacobi-Bellman equation for the joint spread-inventory-hedging control problem under CARA utility with a verification theorem; (iii) High-APY Regime Theorems characterizing profitable regions via five dimensionless parameters, culminating in a Master APY Formula; (iv) analysis of zero-fee economics on decentralized perpetual exchanges with optimal entry-exit thresholds; (v) optimal cross-exchange hedging policies with funding rate dynamics and a hedge regime trichotomy; (vi) a robustness margin quantifying parameter uncertainty tolerance; (vii) exponential drawdown probability bounds and a universal APY-VaR identity; (viii) ergodic inventory distribution under optimal control with Bayesian adaptive estimation; (ix) Kelly-optimal leverage with ruin boundaries; and (x) multi-pair portfolio allocation with diversification saturation results. Numerical analysis with twenty-three figures reveals phase transitions between profitable and unprofitable regimes. Our framework unifies and extends the Avellaneda-Stoikov, Gueant-Lehalle-Fernandez-Tapia, and Glosten-Milgrom paradigms for modern decentralized venue microstructure.

</details>


### [132] [In-Context Reinforcement Learning under Non-Stationarity: A Survey](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11906)
*A Run,Ziluo Ding*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文综述了非平稳环境下的上下文强化学习（ICRL），即策略参数固定时，模型如何通过上下文推断任务规则并适应环境变化，并按照“什么在变化、变化如何展开、变化对智能体的可观测性”三个问题对文献进行组织。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有ICRL综述主要围绕预训练目标、架构、上下文格式、评估协议和理论机制展开，而对非平稳设置的研究相对不足。在变化的环境中，积累的上下文可能变得过时或误导，需要系统梳理这一方向。

Method: 定义了非平稳ICRL问题，将其与元强化学习、决策序列建模、检索增强强化学习、价值/模型感知ICRL及奖励反馈智能体相关联，并按照三个维度（什么在变化、变化如何展开、变化对智能体的可观测性）组织文献。

Result: 指出非平稳ICRL的核心挑战在于策略参数固定时，模型必须同时推断当前决策规则和积累证据中哪些部分仍然有效。

Conclusion: 非平稳ICRL是值得深入研究的开放方向，现有工作尚未完全解决上下文在环境变化时的适应性问题。

Abstract: The development of decision-pretrained transformers, algorithm distillation, long-context meta-RL, and retrieval-augmented agents has renewed interest in in-context reinforcement learning (ICRL): the ability of a pretrained or fine-tuned decision model to infer latent task rules and improve future behavior from interaction context, without test-time parameter updates. This line of work asks when trial-and-error evidence, rewards, transitions, demonstrations, feedback, or retrieved experience can make learning-like computation happen inside the context window. However, existing surveys of ICRL mainly organize the field around pretraining objectives, architectures, context formats, evaluation protocols, and theoretical mechanisms, while the non-stationary setting remains comparatively underexamined. In changing environments, accumulated context is not merely more evidence about a fixed task: the reward specification, transition kernel, observation channel, action interface, constraint model, or demonstration and memory distribution can fall out of alignment with the current regime. Previously useful context can therefore become stale, misleading, or useful again when an old regime returns. We survey non-stationary ICRL as the problem of adapting through context while deployed policy parameters remain fixed: the policy must infer both the current decision rule and which parts of its accumulated evidence still support that rule. We define non-stationary ICRL, relate it to meta-RL, decision sequence modeling, retrieval-augmented RL, value- and model-aware ICRL, and reward-feedback agents, and organize the literature along three questions: what changes, how the change unfolds, and how observable the change is to the agent.

</details>


### [133] [Ontology-Amplified Distillation and Contextuality Auditing for Sovereign Enterprise Language Models: A Combined Proof-of-Mechanism and Negative-Results Method Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11948)
*Thanh Luong Tuan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文结合两项FAOS研究，报告了基于本体增强蒸馏的Qwen学生模型（在47个偏好对、单台M5 Max上训练）在40项越南金融任务中达到与GPT-5相当的接地率（0.90），但统计效力不足无法证明等价或超越；同时报告了企业智能体路由的情境性审计方法（负结果：情境性度量为零）。两项研究均不支持部署、安全、优越性、统计等价或情境性正向路由规则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受数据驻留法规约束的金融机构需要可在机构内部部署的租户自有语言模型，本研究旨在探索一种结合本体增强蒸馏和情境性审计的方法，以在合规前提下构建和评估企业级智能体模型。

Method: 采用本体增强蒸馏方法：以Qwen3.6-27B为学生模型，通过前沿教师轨迹的监督微调和基于本体的直接偏好优化（DPO）进行适配，在单台Apple M5 Max上使用47个合成英语跨领域偏好对训练。同时报告了情境性审计方法，包括情境性-默认校正、直接影响和构造耦合分析。

Result: 蒸馏学生在40项越南金融任务中接地36项（接地率0.90，本体术语覆盖率r_onto=0.95），与GPT-5基线持平。但差异的95%置信区间跨度±4项任务，统计效力不足以建立等价性，且未验证学生超越前沿的预注册预测。情境性审计中，所有Phase 1.3组的情境性-默认校正度量为零，有用信号为直接影响和构造耦合。

Conclusion: 两项研究共同提供了本体接地模型构建机制与治理诊断工具，但证据不支持可部署性、安全性、优越性、统计等价性或情境性正向路由规则；当出现表面分歧时，建议触发提示标准化、多智能体综合或人工审查。

Abstract: Regulated financial institutions operating under data-residency rules need tenant-owned language models that can run inside the institution's perimeter. This paper combines two related FAOS studies into one mechanism-and-control article. First, it reports a reduced-power proof-of-mechanism study of ontology-amplified distillation: a Qwen3.6-27B student is adapted to the Foundation AgenticOS ontology through supervised fine-tuning on frontier-teacher trajectories and ontology-grounded direct preference optimization (DPO), trained locally on a single Apple M5 Max from 47 synthetic, English-language, cross-domain preference pairs. On 40 held-out Vietnamese financial-domain tasks, the distilled student grounds 36 of 40 tasks (grounded rate 0.90; mean ontology term-coverage r_onto = 0.95 on a metric floored at 0.50), equal to the GPT-5 frontier baseline, which also grounds 36 of 40. The outcome is underpowered to establish equivalence: the paired-difference 95% confidence interval spans +/-4 tasks, and the run does not test or show the pre-registered amplification prediction that the student should exceed the frontier. Second, the paper consolidates a contextuality-audit method for enterprise-agent routing. In a separate negative-results pilot, the corrected canonical Contextuality-by-Default degree is zero for all Phase 1.3 groups in both the local-Qwen run and an explicitly labeled Gemma replication check; the useful signal is direct influence and construct coupling, not surviving residual contextuality. Together, the studies pair an ontology-grounded model-building mechanism with a governance diagnostic for deciding when apparent disagreement should trigger prompt standardization, multi-agent synthesis, or human review. The evidence supports neither deployability, safety, superiority, statistical equivalence, nor a contextuality-positive routing rule.

</details>


### [134] [GRID: Grammar-Railed Decoding for Enterprise SQL Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11951)
*Mohsen Arjmandi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: GRID是一个基于语法约束的解码引擎，通过LALR(1)解析器状态驱动精确下一token掩码，为SQL生成提供可证明的语法有效性、基于角色的访问控制、恒定每token开销和合规级审计追踪。在Spider上，0.5B模型提升+13执行准确率，7B模型经修复达94.5%可执行率。Rust内核实现每token掩码中位数3.6-6.7微秒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型生成的SQL在企业部署中需要满足语法有效、权限合规、可证明保证、恒定开销和合规审计等严格需求，而现有方法无法同时提供这些保障。

Method: 提出GRID解码引擎：1) 将掩码键值定义为解析器配置（词法扫描状态×LALR(1)栈）而非token序列；2) 通过字节级trie遍历桥接LLM token与文法终结符，采用上下文无关/相关分裂保证缓存键可靠性；3) 将基于角色的访问控制编译入文法，角色投影限制产生式，模式词表限制标识符终符；4) 提供可测试的四种保证（完备性、正确性、终止性、近恒定每token开销）；5) Rust实现内核，哈希链式审计追踪。

Result: Rust内核每token掩码中位数3.6-6.7微秒（p50和p90优于llguidance，零假拒绝）；每token守卫成本在n=16,000时位置平坦；Spider上0.5B模型获得+13执行准确率提升；7B模型经一次修复达到94.5%可执行率；审计追踪可位级完美重放，100%篡改检测。

Conclusion: GRID证明了将解析器状态作为约束解码核心状态的可行性，在提供多重可证明保证的同时保持高效和实用。同时坦诚指出掩码无法处理分布忠实性、列级RBAC和非LALR(1)语言的局限性及现有开销。

Abstract: Large language models can write SQL, but enterprise deployment demands more than plausible text: outputs must be syntactically valid, must respect per-role and per-schema policy, must carry provable (not best-effort) guarantees, must not slow down as generations grow, and must leave a compliance-grade record of every decision. We present GRID (Grammar-Railed Decoding), a grammar-constrained decoding engine that keys exact next-token masks on parser configurations (lexer scan state x LALR(1) stack) rather than on token sequences, and uses the incrementally advanced LALR(1) parser itself as a viable-prefix oracle. LLM tokens are bridged to grammar terminals by a byte-level trie walk with a context-independent/context-dependent split that makes cache-key soundness hold by construction. Role-based access control is compiled into the language: role projections subset the grammar's productions and schema lexicons restrict identifier terminals, so forbidden verbs and identifiers are unreachable at mask level. Four guarantees (soundness, completeness, termination, and near-constant per-token cost) are stated with explicit preconditions and each paired with a test or benchmark. Rust kernels bring the per-token mask to a 3.6-6.7 us median, ahead of llguidance at p50 and p90 on two tokenizers with zero false rejects; per-token guard cost is position-flat at n=16,000. On Spider, constrained decoding is worth +13 execution-accuracy points at 0.5B, and one checker-guided repair pass over the provably mask-unenforceable residue (column-level policy) lifts a 7B model to 94.5% executable. A hash-chained per-token audit trail replays bit-identically with 100% tamper detection. We state plainly what the mask cannot do (distribution faithfulness, column-level RBAC, non-LALR(1) languages) and where measured cost remains.

</details>


### [135] [Calibration-First Reward-Component Auditing for Reinforcement Learning Control in Smart Greenhouses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11959)
*Yuhui Bie,Guowei Xu,Yaojun Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出GreenLight-Gym框架，通过可复现的校准优先奖励审计方法，将温室强化学习的标量奖励分解为温度、CO2、湿度、蒸汽压差、遮阳帘和执行代理等条件组件，并在模拟训练、设施适应、挑战赛记录和执行器规则蒸馏之间保持可比性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统温室强化学习仅返回单一模拟器奖励值，种植者或控制工程师无法获知策略何时执行加热、增施CO2、通风、湿度管理、遮阳帘或补光等具体操作行为，缺乏对奖励组件的可解释性和可审计性。

Method: 提出校准优先的奖励审计框架，将标量奖励分解为条件温度、CO2、湿度、蒸汽压差(VPD)、遮阳帘和执行代理项；将GreenLight模拟器适配到第二届自主温室挑战赛的实测气候记录；在模拟训练、设施适配、比赛记录和执行器规则蒸馏等环节中保持相同组件的可比较性。

Result: 在GreenLight-Gym中实现了奖励分解与跨场景可比性，能够将相同的奖励组件评分应用于记录的温室数据。

Conclusion: 该框架使得温室强化学习不仅能提供最优策略，还能向种植者解释策略的具体执行行为，增强了AI控制方案的可信度和实用性。

Abstract: Greenhouse reinforcement learning can test climate-control ideas at a speed and scale that is difficult to achieve with crop experiments alone. For smart-greenhouse control, however, a single simulator return is not enough: a grower or control engineer also needs to know when the policy heats, enriches CO2, vents, manages humidity, deploys screens, or uses lamps.We propose a reproducible calibration-first reward audit framework that keeps named greenhouse-control reward components comparable across simulator training, facility-adapted rollouts, logged Autonomous Greenhouse Challenge records, and actuator-rule distillation. In GreenLight-Gym, the framework decomposes the scalar reward into conditional temperature, CO2, humidity and vapor-pressure-deficit, screen, and actuation-proxy terms; adapts GreenLight to the second Autonomous Greenhouse Challenge logged climate traces; and scores the same components on logged greenhouse data.

</details>


### [136] [Optimization Is Not All You Need](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11977)
*Minh Hua,Rita Raley*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文揭示GPT-2到GPT-3等更流畅模型的演进并非单纯的工程进步，而是"优化文化"的最新体现——即相信沿预设轴线的可量化改进足以穷尽价值问题。作者追溯这种信念从预训练、解码、偏好调优到基准测试的整个技术栈，并将其谱系回溯至审计社会，最终指出优化程序可以衡量生成文本的不可能性，却无法区分这种"不可能是错误还是创造"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者试图批判性地审视AI语言模型发展中隐含的价值预设，指出优化文化将语言合法性的权威从传统机构（学院、文法、考官）转移给了损失函数、奖励模型、基准测试和系统提示——一种"执行判断职能却没有判断能力"的装置。

Method: 采用技术谱系学（techno-genealogy）方法，沿技术栈（预训练→解码→偏好调优→基准测试→界面）逐层分析，并将其置于审计社会的更大文化谱系中进行批判性解读。

Result: 发现优化程序的内在局限：它可以量化文本的统计不可能性（improbability），但无法区分这种不可能性是创造性创新还是语法错误——而这一无法做出区分的手段，却在不到五年内获得了设定"合法语言"协议的权威。

Conclusion: 语言合法性的评判权已从传统人文机构（学院、文法书、考官）转移到技术优化装置（损失函数、奖励模型、基准测试、系统提示）手中，这一装置虽然有执行判断的程序外观，却缺乏真正的判断能力，构成了一种评判权威的异化。

Abstract: In 2019, OpenAI released two million GPT-2 outputs-ungrammatical, half broken-to aid the detection of machine-generated text. The alignment that produced their more fluent successors is usually regarded as an engineering achievement; we read it instead as the newest expression of optimization culture: the conviction, older than the technology, that measurable improvement along predefined axes exhausts the question of value. Tracing that conviction through the stack-pretraining, decoding, preference tuning, benchmarking, interface-and back through its genealogy in the audit society, we arrive at the limit: an optimization procedure can measure how improbable a piece of generated text is; it cannot tell whether that unlikelihood is error or invention. A procedure that cannot make that distinction has nonetheless, within half a decade, assumed the authority to set the protocols of legitimate language. Held for centuries by academies and schoolrooms, grammars and examiners, this authority has been given over to loss functions, reward models, benchmarks, and system prompts: an apparatus that executes the office of judgment with no capacity for judging.

</details>


### [137] [LP Mining with LP2Graph: A Use Case for Railway Rescheduling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11980)
*Jörn Maurischat,Nikola Bešinović,Michael Färber*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出LP Mining with LP2Graph方法，通过规范语法将铁路调度领域的LP/MILP公式解析为类型化变量-方程图，并进行同源化、分层聚类和自动标注，最后通过重生成LaTeX和跨求解器验证，建立了客观、可重复的公式分类体系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 铁路重调度领域的建模知识分散在数百篇论文中，使用不兼容的符号体系；传统的叙述性综述仅按词汇而非结构分类，且无法复现模型，缺乏客观、可重复的结构化分类方法。

Method: 提出LP2Graph核心方法：通过规范语法将每个公式表示为类型化变量-方程图；对源文献进行解析、同源化处理，然后进行自底向上的层次聚类（先变量、再约束与目标、最后整体模型结构），并分别按应用领域和求解方法聚类；使用规则引导的自更新分类器对聚类结果进行标注。

Result: 每个聚类的代表模型被重生成为独立的LaTeX文档，并在CBC、HiGHS和Gurobi三个求解器上重新求解，与源论文报告的最优值进行对比验证。

Conclusion: 该方法生成了客观、可重复的变量、约束和模型类型分类体系，为raiLPminer自动化铁路调度模型开发系列工作提供了原理性基础。

Abstract: Like many optimization-driven domains, railway rescheduling relies on Mixed-Integer Linear Programming (MILP), yet the field's modeling knowledge is scattered across hundreds of papers in incompatible notations, and narrative surveys organize it subjectively: they classify models by vocabulary rather than by structure, and reproduce neither. We present LP Mining with LP2Graph, a method that mines the structure of published LP and MILP formulations into a reproducible dataset and an induced taxonomy. Its core, LP2Graph, represents each formulation admitted by its canonical grammar as a typed variable--equation graph derived from a single canonical model; once a source is extracted into that model, everything downstream is deterministic. Each source is parsed into this model, homologized, and clustered bottom-up (over variables, then constraints and the objective, then whole-model structure) and, separately, by application domain and solution approach; the resulting groups are labeled by a rule-seeded, self-updating classifier. We validate the representation rather than assume it: per-cluster representatives are regenerated as independent LaTeX and re-solved across CBC, HiGHS and Gurobi against the optimum reported in the source paper. The outcome is an objective, repeatable taxonomy of variables, constraints and model types: the principled foundation on which our raiLPminer line of automated railway-rescheduling model development builds.

</details>


### [138] [Designing Agent-Ready Websites for AI Web Agents: A Framework for Machine Readability, Actionability, and Decision Reliability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12056)
*Said Elnaffar,Farzad Rashidi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了"智能体就绪网站"设计框架，通过增强网站的结构清晰度、行动提示、证据信号和时间有效性指标，使电商平台更适应AI代理的自主浏览和操作。实验表明，该框架将AI浏览器代理的严格成功率从49.3%提升至89.3%，并显著降低了部分成功率和步骤消耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI代理越来越多地替代人类自主完成电商搜索、比较和购买流程，现有网站设计、SEO和生成式引擎优化指标无法充分评估网站对智能体中介交互的适配能力，缺乏系统性框架来指导网站为AI代理进行优化。

Method: 提出包含三个维度（智能体可解释性、智能体可执行性、智能体决策可靠性）的设计框架，通过受控实验对比人类导向基线网站与智能体就绪版网站，使用GPT-4.1、Gemini-2.5 Flash和Grok-4 Fast三种浏览器代理模型，执行5项任务共300次运行，评估PASS/PARTIAL/FAIL结果、成功率和效率指标。

Result: 智能体就绪网站在150次运行中取得134次PASS（严格成功率89.3%），而基线仅为74次PASS（49.3%）；PARTIAL结果从43降至3；平均步骤数从9.31降至6.49；在产品详情提取、比较和多约束选择任务中提升最为显著。

Conclusion: 增强的结构清晰性、行动线索、证据信号和时间有效性指标能显著提升AI浏览器代理的可靠性和效率，该框架为智能体就绪式网站设计提供了初步实证支持。

Abstract: Online shopping is increasingly shifting toward a model in which AI agents independently search for products, compare options, evaluate constraints, and carry out parts of the purchasing process for users. Website design must now support both human and agent-mediated interaction. This paper introduces the agent-ready website, a design framework for enhancing the readability, interpretability, verifiability, and actionability of e-commerce platforms for AI agents. Existing web design, SEO, and generative engine optimization (GEO) metrics do not fully assess a website's capacity for agent-mediated interaction. The proposed framework is structured around three dimensions agent interpretability, agent executability, and agent decision reliability supported by features such as machine readability, semantic clarity, agent actionability, and contextual decision-reliability signals. The framework is evaluated through a controlled experiment comparing a human-oriented baseline and an agent-ready version of an identical website prototype, with identical catalogs, pricing, stock, and shopping workflows. The evaluation involved five tasks, three browser-agent models (GPT-4.1, Gemini-2.5 Flash, and Grok-4 Fast), and 300 runs, measuring PASS,PARTIAL,FAIL outcomes, strict and functional success rates, error patterns, step counts, and token consumption. The agent-ready website achieved 134 PASS runs out of 150 versus 74 out of 150 for the baseline (strict success rates of 89.3% vs. 49.3%), with the largest gains in product detail extraction, comparison, and multi-constraint selection. It also reduced PARTIAL outcomes from 43 to 3 and lowered the average step count from 9.31 to 6.49. These results provide preliminary evidence that enhanced structural clarity, action cues, evidence signals, and temporal validity indicators can substantially improve the reliability and efficiency of AI browser agents.

</details>


### [139] [Graph Feedback Controls Consensus and Clique Formation in Open-Weight Language-Model Populations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12077)
*Samer Saab,Chaouki Abdallah*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究开放权重多智能体语言模型系统中的惯例形成，通过命名游戏协议分析不同路由策略（阈值相似性与桥接搜索）对共识达成的影响，发现保留历史记忆和桥接策略能有效恢复共识，而阈值相似性路由会加剧碎片化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体语言模型系统中局部交互的运行时间交互图常被视为实现细节而被忽视，但实际对系统的集体行为有重要影响。本研究旨在系统分析不同路由策略如何影响开放权重LM群体的惯例形成与共识达成。

Method: 采用命名游戏协议，使用受限首位token分数在tokenizer安全标签上测量提示条件化的分数状态分布，构建状态相似性图，分离采样标签一致性与潜在状态空间共识。在1.1B-32B参数的开放权重LM群体上进行控制干预实验，对比阈值相似性路由与桥接搜索路由的效果。

Result: 保留伙伴标签证据是必要但不充分条件；阈值相似性路由消除跨盆地暴露并加剧碎片化，而有记忆的桥接搜索路由常能修复碎片化。在三种子混合四模型网格中，阈值相似性在189个设置-种子运行中未产生最终行为或状态共识，而有记忆桥接在14/18次运行中恢复行为共识。Qwen2.5-32B在有记忆全混合设置中达到稳定共识，阈值相似性则在189个设置中均未达成共识。

Conclusion: 路由策略对多智能体LM系统的集体共识形成有决定性影响：保留历史记忆和桥接策略有助于达成共识，而阈值相似性路由导致碎片化加剧。鲁棒性分析表明该定性排序在不同阈值、群体规模和词汇量下保持稳定，早期窗口图能量特征可提供有效诊断。

Abstract: Multi-agent language-model systems increasingly route local interactions, yet the runtime interaction graph is often treated as an implementation detail. We study convention formation in open-weight LM populations spanning 1.1B-32B parameters with a naming-game protocol. Restricted first-token scores over tokenizer-safe labels let us measure prompt-conditioned score-state distributions, construct state-similarity graphs, and separate sampled-label agreement from latent state-space consensus. Across controlled interventions, in the main open-weight repair grids, retained partner-label evidence is necessary but not sufficient: homophilous threshold-similarity routing deletes cross-basin exposure and amplifies fragmentation, while bridge-seeking routing often repairs fragmentation when memory is available. In a three-seed mixed four-model grid, threshold-similarity produces no final behavioral or state consensus in 189 setting-seed runs, whereas state-component and label-disagreement bridges recover final behavioral consensus in 14/18 retained-memory runs. Across homogeneous model populations, retained history generally shifts fragmented dynamics toward consensus; the clearest case is Qwen2.5-32B, which reaches stable behavioral and final state consensus in all 18 retained-history well-mixed settings, while threshold-similarity reaches neither form of consensus in 189 settings. Robustness over state thresholds, population size, and vocabulary size preserves the qualitative ordering, and early-window graph-energy features provide useful within-grid diagnostics.

</details>


### [140] [Operationalising Multi-Dimensional Evaluation for Conversational Agents: A Scalable, Governed Pipeline with Selective Re-evaluation and Model Benchmarking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12085)
*Niranjan Kumar M,Balaji Nagarajan,Karthik Nair,Faysal Satter,Nithin Surendran*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了GenAI Evaluation，一个基于配置管理的自动化评估流水线，用于大规模评估零售对话系统，每天处理约50,000条记录，已评估超过200万次交互，在12,980条人工标注数据上达到0.93的宏F1分数和89%的翻译可接受性准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 零售对话智能体的评估需要超越词汇重叠指标的自动化方法，以评估意图对齐、事实性、帮助性、清晰度、语气和整体响应质量。LLM-as-a-judge方法虽可扩展，但生产部署中存在治理、可复现性、成本、模式一致性、可追溯性和可靠性等挑战。

Method: 提出GenAI Evaluation流水线，通过规范化、分片、异步执行和模式约束的LLM评分处理生产聊天机器人日志。选择性重评估仅处理不完整、格式错误或模式无效的记录，采用模式锁定、版本化配置、验证日志和记录级溯源支持审计性。

Result: 每天处理约50,000条记录，已评估超过200万次交互。使用12,980条分层随机人工标注记录（4名训练标注员）验证，涵盖14个意图、156个子意图、18个主要领域和129个子领域。流水线宏F1得分为0.93，翻译可接受性准确率为89%。

Conclusion: 该方法有效解决了生产环境下的治理、可复现性、一致性和可追溯性问题，在保证评估质量的同时实现了大规模自动化部署。

Abstract: Evaluating retail conversational agents requires methods beyond lexical-overlap metrics to assess intent alignment, factuality, helpfulness, clarity, tone, and overall response quality. Although LLM-as-a-judge methods provide scalable alternatives to human evaluation, production deployment introduces challenges in governance, reproducibility, cost, schema consistency, traceability, and reliability. We present GenAI Evaluation, a governed, configuration-driven pipeline for large-scale evaluation of retail conversational systems. It processes production chatbot logs through normalization, sharding, asynchronous execution, and schema-constrained LLM scoring. The framework evaluates helpfulness, truthfulness, clarity, tone alignment, and translation-specific dimensions. Selective re-evaluation processes only incomplete, malformed, or schema-invalid records, while schema locking, versioned configurations, validation logs, and record-level provenance support auditability. The framework processes approximately 50,000 records daily and has evaluated more than two million interactions. Validation used 12,980 stratified-random human-labeled records from four trained annotators. Classification covered 14 intents, 156 sub-intents, 18 major domains, and 129 sub-domains. The pipeline achieved a macro F1 score of 0.93 and 89% human-acceptability accuracy for translation.

</details>


### [141] [Representing and Generating Levels Over Time through Playtrace Reconstructive Partitioning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12097)
*Emily Halina,Matthew Guzdial*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新颖的"蛋糕"表示方法，用于隐式编码游戏关卡的动态时间信息，并开发了专门针对该表示法的关卡生成算法PRP。在《推箱子》游戏领域与六种先进PCG方法对比，结果表明该方法能生成有效关卡且不牺牲解多样性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有程序化内容生成方法在表示游戏关卡时，往往忽略了游戏作为动态媒介随时间演变的特性，缺少能隐式编码动态信息的领域无关表示方法。

Method: 提出领域无关的"蛋糕"表示法（cake representation）来隐式编码游戏关卡随时间的动态信息；并开发了专门针对该表示法的Playtrace Reconstructive Partitioning（PRP）关卡生成算法。

Result: 在《推箱子》游戏领域与六种最先进的PCG方法对比，该方法能生成有效关卡，且不牺牲解的多样性。

Conclusion: "蛋糕"表示法相比现有表示方法能更简洁地编码游戏的隐式动态特性，从而支持领域无关的关卡生成算法PRP。

Abstract: Video games are a dynamic medium experienced over time. While there are many Procedural Content Generation (PCG) approaches for generating video game levels, they often use representations that abstract away this dynamic nature. In this paper, we introduce a novel, domain-independent ``cake'' representation for game levels over time which implicitly encodes dynamic information. We present a novel level generation approach Playtrace Reconstructive Partitioning (PRP) specifically developed for this cake representation. We compare against six state-of-the-art PCG approaches in the game domain of \textit{Sokoban}, and find that our approach can generate valid levels without sacrificing solution diversity. We believe our cake representation more neatly encodes the implicit dynamic nature of games compared to existing representations, which allows for our domain-agnostic level generation algorithm PRP.

</details>


### [142] [Connected by Construction: Learning Tractable Near-Tour Marginals for Traveling Salesman Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12127)
*Ke Sun,Xinyuan Zhang,Xinwu Qian*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: C2TSP通过直接学习具有结构意义的潜在哈密顿对象（基于有根1-树吉布斯分布），利用隐式微分进行无监督学习，结合平滑Held-Karp层进行度约束校正和证书引导锐化，在保持可解释结构信息的同时实现了强解码性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于学习的TSP方法通常通过解码或搜索后的路径评估性能，但学习过程本身在热力图、指派、构造策略或搜索引导分数等替代空间中进行，掩盖了根本问题：在解码前实际学习到了什么样的哈密顿结构？本文旨在直接回答这一问题。

Method: 提出端到端无监督学习框架C2TSP，基于连接性由构造保证的有根1-树吉布斯分布族；通过隐式微分从无偏TSP成本中学习残差边扰动；平滑Held-Karp层恢复期望度平衡；证书引导锐化推动分布趋向更接近路径的结构。

Result: 实验表明C2TSP在解码性能强的同时保留了可解释的结构信息；消融实验验证了边扰动和证书引导锐化共同改善了路径成本和路径状结构。

Conclusion: 直接学习结构有意义的潜在对象比在替代空间学习后再解码更有效，C2TSP成功弥合了学习目标与最终哈密顿结构之间的鸿沟。

Abstract: Learning-based methods for the traveling salesman problem (TSP) are often evaluated through the tours produced after decoding or search, but the learned object itself frequently lives in a surrogate space such as heatmaps, assignments, construction policies, or search-guidance scores. This hides the fundamental question: what Hamiltonian structure has actually been learned before decoding? In this study, we directly answer this question by learning TSP through a structurally meaningful latent object, rather than leaving most of the Hamiltonian structure to the final decoding stage. Based on a connected-by-construction rooted $1$-tree Gibbs family, we propose an end-to-end unsupervised learning pipeline called \emph{C2TSP}. The pipeline learns residual edge perturbations from unbiased TSP cost through implicit differentiation. For structural correction, a smoothed Held--Karp layer restores expected degree balance, while certificate-guided sharpening further pushes the connected distribution toward more tour-like structures. Experiments show that C2TSP yields strong decoding performance while preserving interpretable structural information. Ablations further verify that edge perturbation and certificate-guided sharpening jointly improve both tour cost and tour-like structure.

</details>


### [143] [The Emerging Paradigm of Geospatial Foundation Models: From Pre-Training to Agentic Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12177)
*Shelley Cazares*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文系统阐述了地理空间基础模型（GeoFMs）的概念，将其分为可微调视觉模型和零样本视觉-语言模型两类，讨论了从性能-成本分析到MLOps生态的实际部署考量，提出了领域专家选择最优适配策略的框架，并展望了以大语言模型为智能协调器的代理式地理空间推理未来方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 利用基础模型推动卫星和航空影像分析的范式转变，将大规模预训练与下游特定任务分离，使领域专家无需承担高昂计算成本即可快速适配模型，同时保障任务的安全性和机密性。

Method: （1）对GeoFMs进行分类：自监督掩码自编码产生的可微调视觉模型，以及对比学习产生的支持零样本开放词汇分析的视觉-语言模型；（2）提出模型适配策略分类法和成本效益选择框架；（3）展望LLM作为智能协调器、GeoFMs作为工具的代理式地理空间推理架构。

Result: 明确了GeoFMs的核心范式转变（职责分离），区分了两类模型的能力边界，给出了实际部署中的性能-成本分析与MLOps生态考量，提出了面向任务的适配策略选择框架。

Conclusion: GeoFMs将推动地理空间分析从感知层走向认知层，通过LLM驱动的代理系统实现自然语言交互下的复杂分析工作流自动化，实现从感知到认知的跨越。

Abstract: The analysis of satellite and aerial imagery has entered a new era with the advent of foundation models. This paper describes the concept of Geospatial Foundation Models (GeoFMs), which are artificial intelligence/machine learning (AI/ML) models pre-trained on massive geospatial datasets through varied methodologies. We first articulate the core paradigm shift that GeoFMs enable: a separation of duties, where large-scale model providers perform the computationally intensive pretraining, allowing domain experts to rapidly fine-tune or prompt these models for specific, mission-critical tasks. This approach democratizes access to state-of-the-art AI/ML while maintaining the security and confidentiality of the downstream task. We then explore the novel capabilities unlocked by different types of GeoFMs, distinguishing between the finetunable vision models produced by self-supervised techniques like masked auto-encoding, and the vision-language models produced by contrastive learning which enable zero-shot tasks like open-vocabulary image analysis. Next, we discuss the practical considerations for operationalizing GeoFMs, from performance-cost analysis to the broader MLOps ecosystem. To that end, we introduce a taxonomy of model adaptation strategies and propose a framework for domain experts to select the most cost-effective adaptation approach for their particular mission set. Finally, we present a forward-looking vision of Agentic Geospatial Reasoning, where Large Language Models act as intelligent orchestrators, leveraging GeoFMs as tools to answer high-level user queries in natural language and automate complex analytical workflows, moving the field from perception to cognition.

</details>


### [144] [Cost-Governed RAG: Unified Per-Tenant Cost Attribution Across Retrieval and Generation in Multi-Tenant LLM Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12188)
*Navnit Shukla*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Cost-Governed RAG架构，通过TurboVec（码本无关向量索引）与多租户LLM网关集成，实现了嵌入、检索和生成成本的统一租户级归因。在100个模拟租户（1000万向量）的实验环境中，端到端成本归因准确率达99.96%，遥测开销低于查询延迟的0.04%，检索基础设施成本相比托管向量数据库服务降低3.1-9.0倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业RAG部署存在关键治理缺口：LLM生成成本按token计量，但检索层（向量内存、相似性计算、嵌入API调用）仍是未归因的共享成本，导致租户间隐形交叉补贴。

Method: 提出Cost-Governed RAG架构，包含：(1) TurboVec——码本无关向量索引，具有确定性闭式内存公式，支持近精确的每租户检索成本计算；(2) 多租户LLM治理网关，创建统一可观测性栈；(3) 三层成本模型，实现嵌入、检索和生成成本的联合租户级归因。

Result: 在Snowpark Container Services上部署，100个模拟租户（1000万向量，对数正态大小分布）下：端到端成本归因准确率99.96%，遥测开销低于查询延迟的0.04%，检索基础设施成本相比托管向量数据库服务降低3.1-9.0倍。

Conclusion: 码本无关量化既能实现确定性的每租户成本归因，又能消除训练量化器中共享码本的泄露面（该发现为探索性结果，受限于第七节所述条件）。

Abstract: Enterprise Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) deployments face a critical governance gap: while LLM generation cost is metered per token, the retrieval layer - vector memory, similarity compute, and embedding API calls - remains an unattributed shared cost, enabling invisible cross-subsidization among tenants. We present Cost-Governed RAG, an architecture that integrates a codebook-oblivious vector index (TurboVec) with a multi-tenant LLM governance gateway, creating a unified observability stack where embedding, retrieval, and generation costs are jointly attributable per tenant. The architecture exploits TurboVec's deterministic, closed-form memory formula to enable near-exact per-tenant retrieval cost calculation - a property unavailable in graph-based indexes with non-linear memory overhead. Deployed on Snowpark Container Services within a cloud data platform's governance boundary, the system achieves 99.96% end-to-end cost attribution accuracy across 100 simulated tenants (10M vectors, log-normal size distribution) with telemetry overhead below 0.04% of query latency. The architecture reduces retrieval infrastructure cost by 3.1-9.0x compared to managed vector database services under the pricing assumptions detailed in Section IV. We formalize a three-layer cost model and demonstrate that codebook-oblivious quantization enables deterministic per-tenant cost attribution while also removing the shared-codebook leakage surface present in trained quantizers - the latter observation being exploratory and subject to the limitations described in Section VII.

</details>


### [145] [A Threshold Exceedance Framework for CBRN Uplift Evaluation in Frontier Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12200)
*Rahul Gupta,Abhinav Mohanty,Payal Motwani,Venkatesh Saligrama,Satyapriya Krishna,Connor Harris,Gary Anthony Ackerman,Brandon Behlendorf,Tom Hobson,Theodore Wilson,Spyros Matsoukas*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种阈值超越标准（TEC）框架，用于评估前沿语言模型是否实质性提升非专家行为者进行CBRN（化学、生物、放射、核）恶意规划的能力。通过大规模实证研究，区分了生成性提升（从零创建计划）和修订性提升（改进现有计划），发现模型辅助计划在放射领域存在实质性提升，其他领域则有限。研究为缓解和部署治理决策提供了依据，并总结了方法论经验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着前沿语言模型能力的提升，政策制定者和模型开发者需要可靠的方法来评估：模型访问是否显著增加了非专家用户进行高后果CBRN恶意规划的能力（相较于仅使用公开工具）。现有评估在非专家定义、威胁范围、基线、评分标准和决策规则上存在不一致，导致跨研究结果难以比较。

Method: TEC框架将提升研究分解为三个独立可执行组件：确定非专家参与者资格、定义CBRN威胁范围、统计估计实质性提升。在大规模实证中采用两种设计：生成性提升（模型协助从零创建计划）和修订性提升（模型协助改进现有计划）。通过领域专家评审估计提升程度。

Result: 模型辅助计划在某些情况下获得了与专家相当的指导性评分，但确认的实质性提升仅限于放射学领域；其他CBRN领域未观察到显著提升。研究揭示了领域异质性。

Conclusion: 研究结果为缓解和部署治理决策提供了依据（而非描述已部署模型行为）。未来CBRN提升评估应强调：预先指定标准、明确基线、区分生成性与修订性提升估计、仔细区分初步筛选信号与确认的风险判定。

Abstract: As frontier language models advance, policymakers and model developers need methods for assessing whether model access materially increases a non-expert actor's ability to plan high-consequence Chemical, Biological, Radiological, or Nuclear (CBRN) misuse relative to public tools alone. Existing CBRN evaluations differ in non-expert definitions, threat scope, baselines, scoring rubrics, and decision rules, making results difficult to compare across studies. We introduce a Threshold Exceedance Criteria (TEC) framework that decomposes an uplift study into independently executable components: determining non-expert participant eligibility, defining the CBRN threat scope for the study, and statistically estimating material uplift. We then operationalize the TEC framework in a large-scale empirical study using a design that determines two forms of uplift: generative (where a model assists plan creation from scratch) and revisionist (where a model assists refinement of an existing plan). The study produced attack plans across the CBRN domains, which we evaluated through subject-matter-expert review to estimate generative and revisionist uplift. Applying the framework, our empirical study revealed domain heterogeneity: under this controlled pre-release evaluation, model-assisted plans sometimes received expert-equivalent instructional ratings, but confirmed material uplift was limited to the radiological domain. These findings informed mitigation and deployment-governance decisions rather than characterizing deployed model behavior. We conclude with methodological lessons for future CBRN uplift evaluations, emphasizing prespecified criteria, explicit baselines, separation of generative and revisionist estimates, and careful distinction between preliminary screening signals and confirmed risk determinations.

</details>


### [146] [Good Benchmarks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12217)
*Ivan Bercovich*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 好的任务应具备正确性、可解性、可验证性、良好规范性和具有有趣难度的特性，最佳任务是用从业者语言描述真实问题，并通过验证结果而非过程的测试来确认。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 定义和阐述何为"好任务"的标准，帮助任务设计者理解高质量任务的特征。

Method: 通过归纳分析提出好任务的五大特征（正确、可解、可验证、良好规范、有趣困难），并进一步说明最佳任务的实现方式。

Result: 提出了鉴别和设计高质量任务的具体标准框架。

Conclusion: 好的任务设计应当关注真实问题、使用专业语言、重视结果验证而非过程检验。

Abstract: Good tasks are correct, solvable, verifiable, well-specified, and hard for interesting reasons. The best tasks describe a real problem an experienced practitioner would recognize, in language a practitioner would use, with tests that verify the outcome rather than the approach.

</details>


### [147] [Rethinking the Evaluation of Harness Evolution for Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12227)
*Yike Wang,Huaisheng Zhu,Zhengyu Hu,Yige Yuan,Zhengyu Chen,Shakti Senthil,Hannaneh Hajishirzi,Yulia Tsvetkov,Pradeep Dasigi,Teng Xiao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文重新审视了LLM智能体自动框架进化的评估方法，发现现有协议存在两个根本问题：1) 框架进化本质上是一种迭代搜索过程，应与简单的测试时搜索基线在匹配的反馈和推理预算下进行比较；2) 搜索和最终评估共享同一基准会导致过拟合风险。实验表明，自动框架进化并不持续优于简单测试时缩放方法，且泛化能力有限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动框架进化方法存在两个根本性评估缺陷：一是框架进化的迭代搜索过程未被与同等计算预算下的简单搜索基线公平比较，导致无法区分性能提升来自更好的框架设计还是额外的搜索；二是搜索与最终评估使用同一基准，存在过拟合风险。因此需要重新审视评估协议。

Method: 将自动框架进化与简单的测试时缩放（test-time scaling）和发现基线在匹配的反馈和推理预算下进行公平比较，同时在保留任务上评估进化后的框架以检验泛化性。实验使用Terminal-Bench 2.1基准，基于GPT-5.4和Claude Opus 4.6模型。

Result: 自动框架进化并未持续优于简单的测试时缩放方法，且进化得到的框架在保留任务上泛化能力有限，无法证明其性能提升源于更好的框架设计而非额外的搜索。

Conclusion: 自动框架进化的有效性受到质疑，需要建立更公平的评估协议和基准来评估自动框架设计方法。

Abstract: We revisit the evaluation of automatic harness evolution for LLM agents. Existing harness evolution methods use unit test cases to search for harness configurations and then report final performance on the same public benchmark. This protocol raises two fundamental concerns. First, harness evolution is itself an iterative search procedure that repeatedly evaluates and revises candidate harnesses using task feedback. As in agentic test-time scaling, it should therefore be compared with simple task-level search baselines under matched feedback and inference budgets to determine whether its gains arise from improved harness design or from additional search alone. Second, because the search and the final evaluation share the same benchmark, the reported gains risk overfitting to that specific task set. To address these concerns, we conduct an extensive evaluation comparing harness evolution with simple test-time scaling and discovery baselines under comparable feedback and inference budgets, and also evaluate evolved harnesses on held-out tasks to assess whether the discovered improvements generalize. Experiments on Terminal-Bench 2.1 with GPT-5.4 and Claude Opus 4.6 show that automatic harness evolution does not consistently outperform simple test-time scaling methods and exhibits limited generalization. Our results raise important questions about the effectiveness of automatic harness evolution and highlight the need for fairer evaluation protocols and benchmarks for automatic harness design. Our code is available at https://github.com/rethinking-harness-evolution.

</details>


### [148] [On-Device Deep Research at 4B: Exposure Bounds Faithfulness, Retrieval Bounds Coverage](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12257)
*Vinay Kumar Chaganti*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究在24GB笔记本上部署4B参数模型作为端侧研究智能体，系统测量了引用忠实性。实验将"引用忠实性"拆分为两个独立指标：被引来源是否支持主张（引用主张忠实性）和模型是否引用正确来源（可信覆盖率）。通过交叉控制每个来源的暴露量（400 vs 1500字符）和来源质量（黄金文献 vs 检索文献），发现暴露量决定忠实性（从0.37/0.45提升至0.58），而检索决定覆盖率（始终约0.22）。增加暴露量仅需约235个额外输出token。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 端侧设备上的研究智能体在笔记本上执行搜索、阅读和写作任务，但其引用是否忠实以及代价高昂，对于可部署的小模型而言尚未被量化测量。现有研究通常将引用的不同维度混为一谈，缺乏对引用忠实性细粒度分析的方法论。

Method: 固定使用4B参数生成器在24GB笔记本上运行，将引用忠实性分解为两个独立指标：引用主张忠实性（被引来源是否支持主张）和可信覆盖率（是否引用了正确来源）。交叉实验设计：控制每个来源的暴露量（400 vs 1500字符）和来源质量（黄金文献 vs 检索文献），使用两个独立评判者评估结果。

Result: 暴露量提升忠实性：检索来源从0.45升至0.58，黄金来源从0.37升至0.58，两者收敛。可信覆盖率在检索来源上始终约0.22，因为召回率维持在约0.40。增加暴露量代价约为235个额外输出token。提升效果经第二个独立评判者验证具有鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 两个杠杆作用于不同指标：暴露量决定引用主张忠实性，检索决定可信覆盖率。实用策略是首先低成本提升每个来源的暴露量，然后将检索召回率作为唯一剩余的杠杆进行优化。

Abstract: On-device research agents search a corpus, read sources, and write a cited brief on a personal laptop. Whether their citations are faithful, and at what cost, is unmeasured for a deployable small model. This study fixes one 4B generator on a 24 GB laptop and asks what makes its citations faithful. It separates two quantities usually reported as one number. Cited claim faithfulness asks whether the cited source supports the claim. Trustworthy coverage asks whether the agent also cites the right sources. The study crosses how much of each source the generator sees, 400 against 1500 characters, with the quality of the sources supplied, gold papers against retrieved papers. Two levers fall out, and they act on different outcomes. Exposure sets faithfulness. More of each source lifts faithfulness from 0.45 to 0.58 on retrieved sources and from 0.37 to 0.58 on gold sources, and the two settings converge, so faithfulness is bound by exposure, not by whether the source is correct. The exposure lift is robust to a second, independent judge; the exact convergence is tight under the primary judge and only approximate under the second. Retrieval sets coverage. Trustworthy coverage stays near 0.22 on retrieved sources at any exposure, because recall is held near 0.40, so exposure cannot fix which sources are cited. The extra exposure costs about 235 output tokens. The practical recipe is to raise per source exposure first, cheaply, and then treat retrieval recall as the only remaining lever.

</details>


### [149] [How Many Tasks Are Enough for Agent Benchmark Decisions? A Replay Analysis of Public LLM Agent Benchmarks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12338)
*Wei-Jung Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究部分预算下代理基准测试的结论稳定性，发现在严格阈值下不同基准所需任务比例差异巨大（15%-90%+），并提出部分评估报告应包含的关键要素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 完整基准测试成本高昂，诱使研究者使用部分运行结果，但部分运行能否支持与完整基准相同的成对比较结论尚不明确。

Method: 回放SWE-bench、AppWorld和tau-bench的完整公开任务级记录，定义部分预算"足够"的条件（支持完整决策、覆盖必要任务组、未解决比较不超过目标比例），在严格0百分点阈值下按5百分点预算网格分析。

Result: AppWorld在15%任务时达标，tau-bench在25%达标，SWE-bench Verified需90%，SWE-bench Lite在95%时仍未达标。

Conclusion: 所需任务比例因基准而异且差异巨大，部分评估报告应明确说明：胜出幅度、任务选择方式、覆盖规则、决策规则及可保留的未解决比较数量。

Abstract: Agent benchmarks often compare two agents after all tasks have run, but costly evaluations make partial runs tempting. A task fraction alone does not show whether a partial run supports the same pairwise conclusion as the completed benchmark. We study this question by replaying completed public task-level records from SWE-bench, AppWorld, and tau-bench. A partial budget counts as enough only when it supports the completed benchmark's decision, covers required task groups, and leaves no more than a target fraction of comparisons unresolved. The required task fraction varies sharply. At the strict 0 percentage point threshold on a 5 percentage point budget grid, AppWorld first meets all targets at 15 percent, tau-bench at 25 percent, and SWE-bench Verified at 90 percent; SWE-bench Lite does not meet all targets by 95 percent under the primary coverage rule. Partial-evaluation reports should state how much one agent must outperform another, how tasks are selected, what coverage rule is required, what decision rule is used, and how many comparisons may remain unresolved.

</details>


### [150] [PM-Bench: Evaluating Prospective Memory in LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12385)
*Genglin Liu,Saadia Gabriel*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出PM-Bench，一个基于文本的前瞻记忆基准测试，用于评估LLM智能体在模拟一周时间内维持用户意图、执行延迟意图和监控环境变化的能力。实验显示，最优方法（GPT-5.4智能体）仅达到65.1% F1分数，且没有单一策略能有效提升所有模型的前瞻记忆能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体AI面临前瞻记忆（Prospective Memory）的重大挑战，即智能体需要在执行其他活动的同时，在特定未来线索或状态下执行一个意图。目前缺乏标准化的基准来评估和诊断LLM智能体的这一能力。

Method: 基于认知科学中的Virtual Week范式，设计了一个为期七天的模拟环境。智能体需在持续进行日常活动的同时，判断是否有延迟任务需要执行。评估了8种最先进的LLM在8种不同智能体配置下的表现。

Result: 所有设置下PM-Bench都具有挑战性，最佳方法（GPT-5.4智能体）仅达到65.1% F1分数。没有单一策略能在所有模型上主导性提升前瞻记忆性能。

Conclusion: PM-Bench可作为诊断前瞻记忆失败的受控测试平台，并为开发支持可靠前瞻行为的训练或推理时干预措施提供基础。当前LLM智能体的前瞻记忆能力存在显著不足。

Abstract: A significant challenge in agentic AI is prospective memory: the ability to execute an intention at a specific future cue or state while other activities are ongoing. We introduce PM-Bench, a text-based benchmark for measuring prospective memory capabilities in modern LLM agents. Inspired by the Virtual Week paradigm from cognitive science, PM-Bench evaluates how well LLM agents maintain user intentions, execute delayed intentions, and monitor latent environment changes. Over the course of a simulated seven-day week, agents must continue an ongoing activity while deciding whether any deferred task is due. We compare eight state-of-the-art LLMs on PM-Bench under eight different agent configurations. PM-Bench proves challenging across all settings: the best method, a GPT-5.4 agent, reaches only 65.1\% F1 score under our evaluation. Furthermore, no single strategy for improving prospective memory dominates across models. We release PM-Bench as a controlled testbed for diagnosing these failures and developing training or inference-time interventions that support reliable prospective behavior.

</details>


### [151] [Critic Experience Bank: Self-Evolving Step-Level Confidence Estimation for LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12397)
*Yaopei Zeng,Congchao Wang,JianHang Chen,Nan Wang,Yurui Chang,Lu Lin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出CONFICL（Conformalized Confidence from In-Context Learning），一种无需训练的自演进批评框架，通过Hindsight LLM为历史步骤生成伪标签并存入记忆库，在相似步骤出现时检索相关经验来估计每个动作的步骤级置信度，相比最强无训练基线将ECE降低最多54%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体在外部环境中执行动作时，每一步错误都可能浪费交互预算或触发不可逆副作用，因此需要在动作执行前获得校准的步骤级置信度估计，而现有的LLM置信度估计方法只关注对给定提示的响应评分，无法捕捉动作执行后的环境反馈后果。

Method: 提出CONFICL框架：1) Hindsight LLM在完整轨迹后回顾每个步骤是否有效，生成伪标签；2) 伪标签存入记忆库；3) 当相似步骤再次出现时，从记忆库中检索相关的成功/失败经验注入Critic的提示中，从而估计当前动作的置信度。

Result: 在三个智能体基准和三个Critic基座上，CONFICL在所有数据集-Critic组合中均取得最优的校准（ECE和Brier）和排序（AUC）性能，相比最强无训练基线ECE降低最多54%。

Conclusion: CONFICL无需训练且无需真实步骤标签，通过自演进的经验积累有效提升了LLM智能体在复杂环境中的步骤级置信度估计能力，为LLM智能体的可靠部署提供了重要支撑。

Abstract: LLM agents act in external environments where each action changes the state that later decisions condition on, and where a single wrong step can waste interaction budget or trigger irreversible side effects long before the final failure is observed. Reliable deployment therefore requires \emph{step-level confidence estimation}: a calibrated probability that each proposed action is productive, available \emph{before} the action is executed. Existing LLM confidence estimators are designed to score a response from the given prompt, but agent confidence also depends on execution consequences: whether similar actions in similar situations actually advanced the task after the environment responded. We introduce the \method (\methodshort), a self-evolving critic framework in which an LLM critic accumulates evidence from its own past judgments and their observed consequences. After each trajectory, a hindsight LLM that sees the full execution feedback votes on whether each step was productive. The resulting pseudo-labels populate a memory bank from which related productive and unproductive experiences are retrieved into the critic's prompt whenever a similar step recurs. \methodshort requires no training and uses no ground truth step labels. Across three agent benchmarks and three critic backbones, \methodshort attains the best calibration (ECE and Brier) and ranking (AUC) in every dataset--critic combination, reducing ECE by up to $54\%$ relative to the strongest training-free baseline.

</details>


### [152] [Isolation as a First-Class Principle for LLM-Agent System Safety: Concepts, Taxonomy, Challenges and Future Directions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12406)
*Huihao Jing,Wenbin Hu,Shaojin Chen,Haochen Shi,Sirui Zhang,Hanyu Yang,Changxuan Fan,Zhongwei Xie,Hongyu Luo,Wun Yu Chan,Wei Fan,Haoran Li,Yangqiu Song*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本综述将隔离（Isolation）作为LLM智能体系统安全的一阶原则，提出以边界为中心的五大边界分类法（用户-智能体、智能体-工具、智能体-执行、智能体-智能体、系统-环境），系统梳理了提示注入、工具误用、记忆投毒等攻击的共享结构成因及其跨边界传播机制，并总结了防御策略与开放挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前关于LLM智能体安全的研究分散在不同的攻击类型、应用场景和基准测试中，缺乏统一的分析框架。作者发现许多看似不同的攻击（如提示注入、工具误用、记忆投毒）实际上共享相同的结构性成因（隔离缺失），且这些攻击会在智能体工作流中跨边界传播。因此，需要一种统一的视角来解释这些现象并指导防御设计。

Method: 作者将隔离（Isolation）作为LLM智能体系统安全的第一原则，提出以边界为中心的（boundary-centric）五边界分类法：(1) 用户-智能体边界；(2) 智能体-工具边界；(3) 智能体-执行边界；(4) 智能体-智能体边界；(5) 系统-环境边界。在此框架下，梳理现有文献，分析每个边界上的隔离失效点、攻击传播路径及相应防御措施，并总结跨边界失效路径。

Result: 建立了一个统一的边界导向的LLM智能体安全分类体系，识别了各边界上隔离丧失的首次发生点、妥协如何在边界间传播，以及每个接口上最相关的防御策略。归纳了跨边界失效路径，并指出开放挑战。

Conclusion: 隔离应是LLM智能体系统安全的设计原则（isolation-by-construction）。未来的研究应致力于在智能体系统的构建阶段就将隔离机制内建其中，而非事后修补。作者提出了相应的研究议程。

Abstract: The capability of LLM agents to function as the ``brain'' of a system fundamentally expands the scope of analysis beyond a standalone model. Consequently, safety is no longer only about input--output content alignment. It also concerns system behavior and real-world execution outcomes. However, the current literature is fragmented across attack types, applications, and benchmarks. This makes it hard to explain why failures such as prompt injection, tool misuse, and memory poisoning often share the same structural cause, and how they spread through an agent workflow. In this survey, we treat isolation as a first-class principle for LLM-agent system safety. By isolation, we refer to the separation of user inputs, tool access, execution channels, inter-agent communication, and environment-originated context. We organize the literature with a boundary-centric taxonomy of five boundaries: user-agent, agent-tool, agent-execution, agent-agent, and system-environment. This view helps identify where the loss of isolation first occurs, how compromise propagates across boundaries, and which defenses are most relevant at each interface. We also summarize cross-boundary failure paths, discuss open challenges, and outline a research agenda for isolation-by-construction in future agent systems.

</details>


### [153] [Accepted Prefixes Are Not All You Need: A Negative Result on PEFT-Based Block-Diffusion Drafting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12422)
*Abdurrahman Javat,Allan Kazakov*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: PEFT-BD是一种基于相同骨干网络的投机解码方法，使用LoRA风格的适配器作为块扩散起草器，虽具有参数高效、避免分词器不匹配等优势，但由于起草器仍需全骨干网络前向传播，导致计算成本与验证器相当，无法实现实际加速。研究表明，投机解码成功的关键条件是起草器必须比验证器计算成本低得多。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索一种参数高效的投机解码方法，通过在同一骨干网络上使用LoRA风格的适配器作为块扩散起草器，避免加载独立起草模型、分词器不匹配等开销，同时利用BD3LM-style的去噪目标并行生成令牌块。

Method: 提出PEFT-BD方法：在相同骨干网络上，使用LoRA-like适配器作为块扩散起草器（block-diffusion drafter），采用BD3LM-style的去噪目标并行生成令牌块，再由原始自回归模型进行验证。

Result: 在Qwen3-0.6B实验中，PEFT-BD虽能获得非平凡的接受前缀长度，但未带来实际加速。原因是每个投机步骤需要两次全骨干网络前向传播：一次启用适配器的起草过程，一次禁用适配器的验证过程，导致起草器计算成本与验证器相当。

Conclusion: 投机解码成功的关键条件是起草器必须比验证器计算成本低得多。参数高效（parameter-efficient）不等于计算高效（compute-efficient），仅靠更长的接受前缀无法弥补起草计算量仍处于验证器规模的问题。

Abstract: Speculative decoding accelerates autoregressive language model inference by using a cheap drafter to propose multiple future tokens and a target model to verify them. A common design goal is therefore to improve draft quality while reducing auxiliary parameters and systems overhead. We study a negative result for this direction through PEFT-BD, a same-backbone speculative decoding method in which a LoRA-like adapter acts as a block-diffusion drafter for an autoregressive verifier. PEFT-BD is motivated by several attractive properties: it avoids tokenizer mismatch, avoids loading a separate draft model, adds only a small number of trainable parameters, and uses a BD3LM-style denoising objective to propose a block of tokens in parallel. Despite these advantages, PEFT-BD does not yield a practical speedup in our Qwen3-0.6B experiments. Although the method obtains nontrivial accepted prefixes, profiling shows that each speculative step requires an adapter-enabled full-backbone draft pass followed by an adapter-disabled full-backbone verification pass. Thus, the drafter is parameter-efficient but not compute-efficient. Our results isolate a simple but important condition for successful speculative decoding: the drafter must be substantially cheaper to execute than the verifier. Longer accepted prefixes alone cannot compensate when draft computation remains verifier-scale.

</details>


### [154] [EVOQUANT: Self-Evolving Verifier-Guided Strategy Optimization for Robust Quantitative Trading](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12455)
*Jie Mao,Changlun Li,Xiang Li,Qiqi Duan,Jinhui Yuan,Xiang Liu,Yuyu Luo,Jing Tang,Xiaowen Chu,Nan Tang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出EVOQUANT，一种基于自进化验证器引导的量化策略优化框架。该方法利用LLM诊断性能瓶颈、生成语义受控的候选策略、通过多阶段验证流水线筛选最优策略，并将优化经验蒸馏为可复用的知识。在A股和加密货币市场的七个代表性策略上，平均测试夏普比率从-0.298提升至0.538，最优策略相对提升199%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量化策略优化目前严重依赖领域专家手动识别弱信号、调整风控规则并反复验证迭代，效率低下且成本高昂。虽然LLM有潜力加速该过程，但直接让其重写策略常导致幻觉编辑、策略漂移和回测过拟合等问题。

Method: 提出EVOQUANT框架，包含四个核心步骤：(1) 利用LLM深度诊断策略性能瓶颈；(2) 生成语义受控的候选编辑策略；(3) 通过多阶段验证流水线筛选最优策略；(4) 将优化经验蒸馏为可复用的知识，实现持续自我改进。

Result: 在A股市场（4个策略）和加密货币市场（3个策略）的七个代表性策略上进行评估，所有策略的夏普比率均显著提升：平均测试夏普比率从-0.298提升至0.538，最优策略实现199%的相对提升。消融实验和压力测试进一步验证了框架的有效性和鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 该工作将量化策略优化从昂贵的人工试错转变为自动化、可验证的迭代范式，为LLM在金融策略研究中的应用提供了新路径。

Abstract: Quantitative strategy optimization remains largely manual, requiring domain experts to identify weak signals, tune risk-control rules, and repeatedly validate iterative revisions. Large language models can accelerate this process, but directly relying on them to rewrite trading strategies often introduces hallucinated edits, strategy drift, and backtest overfitting. We propose EVOQUANT, a self-Evolving Verifier-guided framework for strategy Optimization in Quantitative trading. Our method utilizes LLMs to deeply diagnose performance bottlenecks, generates semantically controlled candidate edits, selects the best strategy through a multi-stage verification pipeline, and distills optimization experience into reusable knowledge for continual self-improvement. We evaluate our method using seven representative strategies: four from the A-share market and three from the Crypto market. Experimental results show that our method significantly improves the Sharpe ratio across all tested strategies: the average test Sharpe increases from -0.298 to 0.538, and the best-performing strategy achieves a 199% relative improvement. Ablation studies and stress tests under stricter conditions further validate the effectiveness and robustness of the framework. Overall, this work transforms quantitative strategy optimization from costly manual trial and error into an automated and verifiable iterative paradigm, offering a new path for applying large language models to financial strategy research.

</details>


### [155] [Do We Really Need Transformers for Global Spatial Information Extraction in Traffic Forecasting?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12462)
*Qihang Zhang,Siyao Zhang,Letao Kang,Wenzhe Liang,Miao Zhang,Zhao Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过可控消融框架比较了全局注意力机制与简单全局聚合算子（均匀全范围混合）在交通预测中的表现，发现两者性能差异仅0.14%，且均匀混合计算复杂度从O(N²)降至O(N)。进一步分析表明，空间注意力可分解为行均匀全局背景和非均匀残差，后者贡献边际，提示使用空间注意力需有稳定增益证明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有交通预测模型普遍关注全局空间依赖提取，但对全局信息建模与提取的底层机制研究不足，尤其是不清楚全局信息必须通过高自由度自适应注意力提取，还是可由简单的全局聚合算子捕获。

Method: 设计可控消融框架，仅替换空间混合模块以测试基于注意力的全局交互；将空间注意力分解为行均匀全局背景和非均匀残差进行分析。

Result: 在六个交通基准上，均匀全范围混合与标准空间注意力各有三个数据集取得更低MAE，均值差异仅0.14%，且均匀混合将节点级空间混合复杂度从O(N²)降至O(N)。非均匀残差贡献呈数据集依赖的边际价值。

Conclusion: 空间注意力应仅在有稳定增益超过行均匀全局背景的情况下使用，简单的全局聚合算子在多数场景下可作为高效替代。

Abstract: Existing traffic forecasting models commonly focus on extracting spatial dependencies, particularly global spatial information, which characterizes the representations obtained through interactions between each individual node and all nodes across the traffic network. However, the underlying mechanism by which such global information is modeled and extracted remains insufficiently investigated. Whether global information must be extracted by high-degree-of-freedom adaptive attention or can be captured by a simple global aggregation operator remains unclear. For this purpose, we design a controlled ablation framework that replaces only the spatial mixing module to test attention-based global interaction. Across six traffic benchmarks, uniform full-range mixing and standard spatial attention each achieve lower MAE on three datasets, with only a 0.14% difference in mean MAE, while the former reduces node-scale spatial mixing complexity from O(N2) to O(N). Mechanism analysis further decomposes spatial attention into a row-uniform global background and a non-uniform residual. The residual shows dataset-dependent marginal value, suggesting that spatial attention should be justified by stable gains beyond a row-uniform global background. The corresponding source code is publicly available at: https://github.com/uuesti/U-Trans

</details>


### [156] [Function-Aware Fill-in-the-Middle as Mid-Training for Coding Agent Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12463)
*Yubo Wang,Jiarong Liang,Yuxuan Zhang,Xuye Liu,Cong Wei,Yuyu Zhang,Ping Nie,Wenhu Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于函数感知的填充中间训练（FIM mid-training）方法，利用代码中天然存在的函数调用结构（调用者-被调用者-下游消费）来增强编码智能体的推理能力。通过在Qwen2.5-Coder-Instruct (7B/14B) 和 Qwen3-8B 上进行2.6B token的中间训练，该方法在SWE-Bench系列基准上获得显著提升（+2.8~+5.4），并且能够缓解智能体后训练带来的能力退化，包括非编码工具使用基准（tau-bench, BFCL）也获得收益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 编码智能体需要将外部工具返回结果整合到持续推理中，但标准从左到右的预训练只暴露了正向推理能力，缺乏对函数调用这种"调用-返回-消费"结构的学习，限制了智能体在行动-观察-继续循环中的表现。

Method: 通过程序依赖图分析选取函数，结合复杂度和可推断性双重标准，对选中的函数进行填充中间训练（FIM），使模型学习函数调用的调用者-被调用者-下游消费的结构关系；然后在2.6B token的去污染语料（来自968个GitHub仓库）上进行中间训练，再应用现有智能体后训练流程。

Result: 在SWE-Bench-Verified上+2.8/+3.0 (7B/14B)，+3.2 (Qwen3-8B)；SWE-Bench-Lite上+3.7/+4.0/+5.4；效果在两个后训练流程（R2E-Gym, SWE-Smith）及非Qwen2.5基座上均保持；同时缓解了智能体后训练对非编码任务（LiveCodeBench, tau-bench, BFCL）的能力侵蚀，尽管中间训练语料仅包含Python代码。

Conclusion: 函数感知的FIM中间训练能够有效注入函数调用归纳偏置，提升编码智能体的工具使用推理能力，且该偏置能泛化到非编码工具使用场景，具有较好的跨任务迁移性。

Abstract: Coding agents must integrate external tool returns into ongoing reasoning - a capability that standard left-to-right pretraining on code exposes only in its forward direction. We observe that the action-observation-continuation loop of a coding agent is structurally isomorphic to a function call site, where a caller binds arguments, a callee returns a value computed elsewhere, and downstream code consumes that value. This conditioning structure exists at internet scale in ordinary code. We exploit it through function-aware fill-in-the-middle (FIM) mid-training: a self-supervised objective that masks functions selected via program dependency graph analysis and a complexity-inferability double criterion. We mid-train Qwen2.5-Coder-Instruct (7B/14B) and Qwen3-8B on a 2.6B-token decontaminated corpus drawn from 968 GitHub repositories, then apply existing agentic post-training pipelines. Mid-training improves SWE-Bench-Verified by +2.8/+3.0 at 7B/14B and by +3.2 on Qwen3-8B; SWE-Bench-Lite gains are +3.7/+4.0/+5.4 on the same models. The improvement holds across two post-training pipelines (R2E-Gym, SWE-Smith) and on a non-Qwen2.5 base (Qwen3-8B with SWE-Lego). Beyond in-domain gains, mid-training also mitigates the capability erosion that agentic post-training otherwise inflicts on non-agent coding (e.g., LiveCodeBench) and non-coding tool-use benchmarks (tau-bench, BFCL): although the mid-training corpus contains Python code only, the function-call inductive bias survives post-training and yields consistent gains.

</details>


### [157] [From Observation to Insight: Mechanistic World Models and the Quest for Autonomous Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12474)
*Ingmar Posner,Anson Lei,Bernhard Schölkopf*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出机械论世界模型（Mechanistic World Models）作为AI实现自主科学发现的新范式，强调以可复用解释性机制为核心，将科学发现重新定义为知识组织问题，并整合机械论可解释性、因果表征学习、方程发现和模块化架构等研究方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI在科学领域虽取得显著预测性能，但预测不等于科学发现。科学理解依赖于发现产生观测的可复用解释性机制，而当代机器学习仍以预测映射而非解释性结构为根本组织方式。

Method: 从科学哲学中汲取洞见，推导科学发现所需的计算能力，识别鼓励解释性知识涌现的设计原则和归纳压力，形式化机械论世界模型的解剖结构，并展示机械论可解释性、因果表征学习、方程发现和模块化架构如何构成该范式的互补要素。

Result: 提出了机械论世界模型作为概念基础和计算蓝图，将科学发现重新定义为知识组织问题，并指出多个研究方向虽捕获了该范式的互补要素但缺乏统一框架。

Conclusion: 机械论世界模型有望推动AI从预测性预测走向自主科学发现，为AI for Science提供超越预测性能的新范式。

Abstract: Recent advances in foundation models have transformed AI for Science, enabling remarkably accurate predictive performance across domains ranging from protein folding to weather forecasting. Yet prediction alone does not constitute scientific discovery. Scientific understanding depends on uncovering the reusable explanatory mechanisms that generate observations, whereas contemporary machine learning remains fundamentally organised around predictive mappings rather than explanatory structure. In this paper, we argue that scientific discovery is fundamentally a problem of knowledge organisation. To this end, we introduce Mechanistic World Models, a new design paradigm that places reusable mechanisms at the centre of representation, computation and learning. Drawing on insights from the philosophy of science, we derive the computational capabilities required for discovery, identify the design principles and inductive pressures that encourage explanatory knowledge to emerge, and formalise the anatomy of a mechanism-centric world model. Finally, we show how diverse research directions including mechanistic interpretability, causal representation learning, equation discovery and modular architectures capture complementary ingredients of this paradigm while lacking a unified framework. We propose Mechanistic World Models as a conceptual foundation and computational blueprint for moving AI beyond predictive forecasting towards autonomous scientific discovery.

</details>


### [158] [TRACE: An Operational Reasoning Schema for Auditable Agentic Commitments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12480)
*Edward Y. Chang,Emily J. Chang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出TRACE——一个带类型、版本化的推理记录模式，包含参考写入程序和"无记录不持久变更"的操作纪律，旨在解决语言模型缺乏形式化推理机制的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 论文从三个层次论证推理不在语言模型中：自回归机制本质上是计算关联；思维链和强化学习继承了这一局限；从苏格拉底方法到Pearl阶梯的推理理论的形式化构造作为机制缺失。因此需要一个模式来记录和审计推理过程。

Method: 提出了TRACE模式，包括TraceRecord及其因果特化、八阶段参考写入器、gate-first测量制度、TRACE-Bench协议，以及记忆准入、计划门控、时间遗憾和裁决复用等消费者组件。定义了记录-消费者契约，使模式成为操作接口而非被动文档。

Result: 通过两个工作示例展示：音乐课论证（从句子到类型化裁决，分离关联、干预和处方）和洪水搜索救援场景（世界模型报告与支持证据和分布外分数矛盾，记录推迟承诺、请求有限观察、仅追加修订并切换分支）。

Conclusion: 贡献在于模式及其契约本身，而非性能声明；闭环评估留待未来工作。附录包含完整模式、写入算法和成本模型、临床和政策示例、基准协议、收敛度量和使用场景。

Abstract: This paper defines TRACE (Typed Reasoning And Commitment Evidence): a typed, versioned schema for recording reasoning traces, a reference procedure for writing records against it, and one operating discipline, no durable state change without a record. The paper argues in three layers that reasoning is not in the language model: the autoregressive mechanism natively computes association; chain-of-thought and reinforcement learning inherit its limits; and the formal constructs of reasoning theory, from Socratic procedure to Pearl's ladder, are absent as machinery. The schema answers the absence with fields and tests: the TraceRecord and its causal specialization, an eight-stage reference writer, a gate-first measurement regime, the TRACE-Bench protocol, and the consumers, memory admission, plan gating, temporal regret, and verdict reuse, whose more auditable decisions are the measure of the record. A record-consumer contract states what a record guarantees and what a consumer must honor in return, making the schema an operational interface rather than a passive document.
