### [190] [Medical Image Segmentation based on Deep Active Contour and Mean Curvature Loss Function](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12586)
*Xiao-qiang Zhai,Zhi-feng Pang,Peng Zheng,Ze-wen Li,Yan-zhe Hou*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 针对医学图像分割中缺乏几何先验信息的问题，本文提出了一种结合平均曲率几何约束的DACMC损失函数，通过卷积核近似计算平均曲率，在肝脏和脾脏数据集上取得了最先进的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度学习方法在像素级训练中缺乏几何先验信息，虽然已有研究将Chan-Vese模型融入损失函数以考虑分割区域内外区域和长度，但仍缺乏对分割区域的有效表征。

Method: 引入平均曲率作为几何自然约束，提出DACMC损失函数，并使用卷积核近似平均曲率以节省计算成本。

Result: 在肝脏和脾脏数据集上验证了方法的有效性，在多个分割数据集上实现了新的最先进性能。

Conclusion: 通过引入平均曲率约束，DACMC损失函数有效增强了分割区域的几何表征能力，在医学图像分割任务中取得了显著性能提升。

Abstract: Medical image segmentation is a crucial task in the field of clinical analysis and applications. Though deep learning techniques recently play a crucial role in several scenarios, the training at the individual pixel level leads to a lack of geometric prior information. Scholars proposed to integrate the Chan-Vese model into the loss function for training which can take into account the region and length of the region inside and outside the segmentation process and then improve the performance in medical image segmentation. However, these methods still lack an effective characterization of the segmented region. To overcome this problem, we introduce the mean curvature as a geometric natural constraint and propose a Deep Active Contour and Mean Curvature (DACMC) loss function where the convolution kernel is used to approximate the mean curvature to save computational cost. We have validated the performance of our method on the liver and spleen dataset. Our proposed method demonstrates new state-of-the-art performance on several segmentation datasets.

</details>


### [191] [Improving Autonomous Nano-drones Performance via Automated End-to-End Optimization and Deployment of DNNs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12593)
*Vlad Niculescu,Lorenzo Lamberti,Francesco Conti,Luca Benini,Daniele Palossi*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 本文探索了在Crazyflie 2.1纳米无人机上自动化部署视觉CNN导航模型（PULP-Dronet）的方法与工具，相比手工优化版本实现了2倍内存缩减、1.6倍推理加速，避障峰值制动速度达1.65 m/s，自由飞行速度达1.96 m/s，计算功耗仅占无人机功率预算的1.6%，并开源了完整项目。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纳米无人机（<10cm）被视为下一代 ubiquitous 智能传感器和机器人助手，但其有限的计算/存储资源使得部署视觉CNN面临巨大挑战；传统迭代开发流程依赖人工且易出错，亟需自动化工具链简化部署过程。

Method: 探索了自动化部署流程和软件工具，在基于ULP多核SoC的Crazyflie 2.1任务计算机上部署PULP-Dronet CNN，覆盖从初始训练到闭环评估的完整流程；通过自动化优化实现模型压缩与加速。

Result: 相比手工CNN实现：内存占用减少2倍，推理速度提升1.6倍，精度保持不变；实际飞行表现：避障峰值制动速度1.65 m/s，熟悉环境自由飞行速度1.96 m/s（基线0.5 m/s），支持含90度转弯的跑道跟随；计算功耗仅占无人机总功率预算的1.6%。

Conclusion: 所提自动化工具链可有效替代劳动密集型的手工迭代开发，在纳米无人机平台上实现高效、高精度的视觉导航部署，为未来应用和研究提供开源基础。

Abstract: The evolution of energy-efficient ultra-low-power (ULP) parallel processors and the diffusion of convolutional neural networks (CNNs) are fueling the advent of autonomous driving nano-sized unmanned aerial vehicles (UAVs). These sub-10 cm robotic platforms are envisioned as next-generation ubiquitous smart-sensors and unobtrusive robotic-helpers. However, the limited computational/memory resources available aboard nano-UAVs introduce the challenge of minimizing and optimizing vision-based CNNs -- which to date require error-prone, labor-intensive iterative development flows. This work explores methodologies and software tools to streamline and automate all the deployment of vision-based CNN navigation on a ULP multicore system-on-chip acting as a mission computer on a Crazyflie 2.1 nano-UAV. We focus on the deployment of PULP-Dronet, a state-of-the-art CNN for autonomous navigation of nano-UAVs, from the initial training to the final closed-loop evaluation. Compared to the original hand-crafted CNN, our results show a 2x reduction of memory footprint and a speedup of 1.6x in inference time while guaranteeing the same prediction accuracy and significantly improving the behavior in the field, achieving: i) obstacle avoidance with a peak braking-speed of 1.65 m/s and improving the speed/braking-space ratio of the baseline, ii) free flight in a familiar environment up to 1.96 m/s (0.5 m/s for the baseline), and iii) lane following on a path featuring a 90 deg turn -- all while using for computation less than 1.6% of the drone's power budget. To foster new applications and future research, we open-source all the software design in a ready-to-run project compatible with the Crazyflie 2.1

</details>


### [192] [Spatially-Aligned Chroma from Luma Prediction for Lossless JPEG XS Raw Image Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12636)
*Taizo Suzuki,Soma Yokota,Masaki Onuki*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于Chroma from Luma (CfL)增强的Star-Tetrix变换（CfL-STT），通过空间对齐的亮度插值样本预测色度分量，在JPEG XS无损原始图像压缩中持续提升编码效率并保持精确可逆性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统CfL预测直接应用于原始图像压缩时，由于缺乏空间对齐，结果依赖于图像特性，甚至可能降低编码效率。因此需要一种针对CFA采样原始图像的CfL预测方法，以有效抑制高频传感器噪声并保持跨通道相关性。

Method: 将CfL预测集成到Star-Tetrix变换（STT）中，通过沿水平和垂直方向进行线性插值，获得空间对齐的亮度样本以匹配色度采样网格，从而在CFA采样原始图像中实现从亮度到色度分量的预测。

Result: 直接应用CfL预测会因缺乏空间对齐而产生图像依赖的性能，并可能降低编码效率；而所提CfL-STT在JPEG XS无损原始图像压缩中持续提升编码效率，同时保持精确的可逆性。

Conclusion: 所提出的CfL-STT通过空间对齐的亮度插值有效地抑制了高频传感器噪声并保留了跨通道相关性，生成了更加去相关的Y-Delta-Du-Dv色彩空间，在无损压缩场景下具有一致且可靠的编码性能增益。

Abstract: This study proposes a Chroma from Luma (CfL)-enhanced Star-Tetrix transform (STT), referred to as CfL-STT, for improving raw image compression in JPEG XS. The proposed CfL-STT integrates CfL prediction into the STT to predict chroma components from the luma component in CFA-sampled raw images. Unlike conventional CfL prediction designed for full-color images, the proposed method employs spatially aligned luma samples obtained via linear interpolation along the horizontal and vertical directions to match the chroma sampling grid. This spatial alignment suppresses high-frequency sensor noise while preserving cross-channel correlation, resulting in a more decorrelated Y-Delta-Du-Dv color space. The proposed method was implemented in the JPEG XS reference software and evaluated on raw image datasets. Experimental results demonstrate that a direct application of CfL prediction yields image-dependent performance and may degrade coding efficiency due to the lack of spatial alignment, whereas the proposed CfL-STT consistently improves coding efficiency in lossless raw image compression while preserving exact reversibility.

</details>


### [193] [Exact and Calibrated Diffusion Reconstruction for Digital Breast Tomosynthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12937)
*Imade Bouftini*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 针对有限角度数字乳腺断层合成（DBT）重建中条件扩散先验存在的三个临床障碍（非精确数据一致性、未定位的幻觉、未校准的不确定性），本文提出通过精确欧几里得投影替代近端更新，实现数据一致性的严格满足，并通过等温重校准实现不确定性校准，首次实现了数据一致、不确定性校准的有限角度DBT学习重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 条件扩散先验在有限角度DBT中虽能实现强感知质量，但存在三个临床障碍：非精确数据一致性、未定位的幻觉、未校准的不确定性，亟需解决这些问题以实现可靠的临床重建。

Method: (1) 用精确欧几里得投影替代条件扩散采样器的每步近端更新，通过m维对偶系统和一次性Gram矩阵AA^T分解实现，每步仅4.5ms（248倍加速）；(2) 证明该投影是近端步骤ρ→0的极限，并提供无伤害定理；(3) 证明精确一致样本集的方差支持在零空间null(A)上；(4) 等温重校准将集成散布校准到误差尺度；(5) 修复部署投影仪中20.3%的伴随不匹配。

Result: (1) 投影每步4.5ms（248倍加速），数据残差降至双精度下限2.4×10^{-13}；(2) 在患者衍生乳腺体模上提升了保真度且不损失深度分辨率；(3) 近端步骤后应用会降低质量，表明一致性步骤的放置位置至关重要；(4) 等温重校准使ECE从0.029降至0.008，标准化误差从4.7降至0.96，误差排序优于纯先验；(5) 修复了20.3%的伴随不匹配。

Conclusion: 本文首次实现了数据一致、不确定性校准的有限角度DBT学习重建，求解器可自然松弛到噪声测量下的不一致球和最大后验模式。

Abstract: Limited-angle digital breast tomosynthesis (DBT) reconstructs a volume from a few low-dose projections over a narrow arc. At a representative nine-view, $25^{\circ}$ protocol more than 98% of image space is unmeasured, so a learned prior must supply structure in the missing wedge. Conditional diffusion priors achieve strong perceptual quality here but leave three clinical obstacles: inexact data consistency, unlocalized hallucination, and uncalibrated uncertainty. We enforce measurements exactly by replacing the per-step proximal update of a conditional diffusion sampler with exact Euclidean projection onto the data-consistent set, computed via an $m$-dimensional dual system with a one-time Gram matrix $AA^{\top}$ factorization. This projection costs 4.5 ms per step (a $248\times$ speedup) and drives the data residual to the double-precision floor ($2.4\times10^{-13}$). We prove it is the $ρ\to0$ limit of the proximal step, provide a no-harm theorem, and show that exactly consistent sample ensembles have variance supported on null($A$). Thus, the mean's entire error lies in the unmeasured subspace covered by the uncertainty map. On patient-derived breast phantoms, this improves fidelity at no depth-resolution cost. Conversely, a proximal step applied post-update degrades quality, isolating the consistency step's placement as decisive. Isotonic recalibration brings the ensemble spread to a calibrated error scale (expected calibration error $0.029\to0.008$; standardized error $4.7\to0.96$), ranking errors better than the pure prior. We also repair a 20.3% adjoint mismatch in a deployed projector via a materialized operator of record. This is the first data-consistent, uncertainty-calibrated learned reconstruction for limited-angle DBT. The solver naturally relaxes to discrepancy-ball and maximum-a-posteriori modes for noisy measurements.

</details>
